I mean, I’m not completely sure what all counts as “undoing,” but I haven’t noticed myself doing things like that. I mostly just… try not to make a bigger deal of it afterward, if that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: During these nighttime eating episodes, do you feel like you can’t stop eating or control what or how much you’re eating, even if you want to?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think that’s a fair way to put it. I don’t mean I’m completely out of control all the time, but late at night, once I start picking at the leftovers, there’s this sensible part of me saying, “That’s enough, Alejandro.” And I do mean it.\n\nBut then… my hand just keeps going back for more anyway. It’s embarrassing to admit, but in that moment it doesn’t feel like I’m choosing very well, if that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: When you’re having these nighttime eating episodes, do you feel like you can't stop eating or control what or how much you’re eating, even if you want to?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think so.
